WITNESS_READWITNESS-READ(the_sons_of_joseph, manasseh_first_machir_begot_gilead_the_daughters_row)
= WITNESS-READ(the-sons-fowl-joseph, manasseh-first-machir-begot-gilead-the-daughters-row)
THE SONS OF JOSEPH — MANASSEH BEFORE EPHRAIM WHERE CHAPTER 1 AND JACOB'S HANDS PUT EPHRAIM FIRST; MACHIR BEGOT GILEAD — THE ROSTER'S ONE 'BEGOT', GILEAD A MAN AT THIS SEAT ALONE; THE SIX OF GILEAD NO GENESIS ROSTER NAMES; ZELOPHEHAD HAD NO SONS, ONLY DAUGHTERS — THE CASE'S PREMISE AS A CENSUS ROW, THE FIVE NAMED HERE FIRST; 52,700 — THE LARGEST RISE [claim PN26A-06]. THE SONS OF JOSEPH — MANASSEH BEFORE EPHRAIM WHERE CHAPTER 1 AND JACOB'S HANDS PUT EPHRAIM FIRST; MACHIR BEGOT GILEAD — THE ROSTER'S ONE 'BEGOT', GILEAD A MAN AT THIS SEAT ALONE; THE SIX OF GILEAD NO GENESIS ROSTER NAMES; ZELOPHEHAD HAD NO SONS, ONLY DAUGHTERS — THE CASE'S PREMISE AS A CENSUS ROW, THE FIVE NAMED HERE FIRST; 52,700 — THE LARGEST RISE. 'The sons of Joseph by their families: MANASSEH and Ephraim' (26:28) — 1:32-35, chapter 2 and Genesis 48:20 ('he set Ephraim before Manasseh') put Ephraim first; Genesis 48:5 made the pair 'mine, as REUBEN AND SIMEON' — the roster's first two (computed); 'and Machir BEGOT Gilead' (26:29) — the roster's one full 'begot' (26:58's is spelled short), Gilead a PERSON here alone ('son of Gilead' 27:1, Josh 17:3, 1 Chr 5:14), Machir born on Joseph's knees (Gen 50:23); Iezer bare of its preposition (26:30); Joseph's TWELVE families (Manasseh eight, Ephraim four) have no Genesis 46 name at all — Joshua 17:2 runs Gilead's six in this order (computed); 'and Zelophehad son of Hepher HAD NO SONS, ONLY DAUGHTERS; and the names of the daughters of Zelophehad: Mahlah, Noah, Hoglah, Milcah and Tirzah' (26:33) — the clause at Josh 17:3 the run ('only daughters' also of Sheshan and Eleazar son of Mahli, 1 Chr 2:34, 23:22); THE DAUGHTERS' FIRST SEAT IN THE INK, before the plea — the fact the case of 27 turns on is a ROW of this census, and 27:1's lineage is 26:29-33 read upward, seven generations from Joseph; 26:33's order = 27:1's = Josh 17:3's, 36:11 reorders (computed); 'two and fifty thousand and seven hundred' (26:34) — 32,200 → 52,700 (+20,500), the largest rise, the smallest tribe of the first census the seventh of this (computed). 		—Onkelos Num 26:28, Onkelos Num 26:29, Onkelos Num 26:30, Onkelos Num 26:31, Onkelos Num 26:32, Onkelos Num 26:33, Onkelos Num 26:34witnessed

THE TWO ROLLS: THESE ARE THE COUNTED OF MOSES AND ELEAZAR IN THE PLAINS OF MOAB — AND AMONG THESE NOT A MAN OF THE COUNTED OF MOSES AND AARON IN THE WILDERNESS OF SINAI: THE MEMBERSHIP PREDICATE; THEY SHALL SURELY DIE IN THE WILDERNESS — THE DECREE'S WORDS; NOT A MAN LEFT — THE LOCUSTS' CLAUSE; EXCEPT CALEB AND JOSHUA — 14:30 VERBATIM. 26:63 and 26:64 are ONE SENTENCE TWICE — 'these are the counted of Moses and ELEAZAR the priest, who counted the children of Israel IN THE PLAINS OF MOAB BY THE JORDAN OF JERICHO' / 'and among these there was NOT A MAN of the counted of Moses and AARON the priest, who counted the children of Israel IN THE WILDERNESS OF SINAI' — the priest and the place the only deltas (computed word by word): two rolls, two takers, two places, and the predicate that no name is on both — the ark's 'all that came out' (Gen 9:10) at national scale, a table checked against a table; 'in the plains of Moab' nine Bible seats, 'by the Jordan of Jericho' seven (all Numbers), 'in the wilderness of Sinai' nine; 'Moses and Aaron' in this chapter here alone; 'not a MAN' — the counted were males twenty and up (computed); 'for the LORD had said of them: THEY SHALL SURELY DIE IN THE WILDERNESS' (26:65, one seat) — 14:35's decree ('in this wilderness they shall be consumed and there they shall die') and 14:29's 'all your counted ones from twenty years old and upward' naming the first roll in the sentence; 'and there was not left of them a man' — 'and was not left' in the Torah here and at the locusts, 'nothing green was left' (Exod 10:15); 'EXCEPT Caleb son of Jephunneh and Joshua son of Nun' — 14:30's last seven words VERBATIM (32:12 'save Caleb', Deut 1:36 'except Caleb'), and the Sifrei's 'only' excludes the same two from the lot (132:3); Deut 2:14-16 the retelling, thirty-eight years 'until all the generation of the men of war was consumed' — the parser reads 38 (computed). 		—Onkelos Num 26:63, Onkelos Num 26:64, Onkelos Num 26:65witnessed
